void __fastcall sub_180045C8F(_DWORD *a1, _DWORD *a2)
{
  _DWORD *v2; // rcx
  _DWORD *v3; // rdx

  *a1 = *a2;
  v2 = a1 + 1;
  v3 = a2 + 1;
  *v2 = *v3;
  v2[1] = v3[1];
  JUMPOUT(0x18004550ALL);
}

// FUNC 180045db4 __stdio_common_vsprintf

        char *Buffer,
        size_t BufferCount,
        const char *Format,
        _locale_t Locale,
